File: src/windows/Feed/components/post/PostCard.jsx
================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useId } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { supabase } from '../../../../lib/supabaseClient';
import { getProfile } from '../../../../lib/profilesCache';
import PostHeader from './PostHeader';
import PostMedia from './PostMedia';
import PostFooter from './PostFooter';
import PostContextMenu from './PostContextMenu';
import Lightbox from '../media/Lightbox';
import HtmlPreviewScreen from '../media/HtmlPreviewScreen';
import './PostCard.css';

export default function PostCard({
  post,
  currentUser,
  onOpen,
  readOnly = false,
  onRequireAuth,
  onOpenProfile,
}) {
  const [views, setViews] = useState(post.views || 0);
  const [likes, setLikes] = useState(0);
  const [isLiked, setIsLiked] = useState(false);
  const [burst, setBurst] = useState(false);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState(null); 
  const [htmlPreview, setHtmlPreview] = useState(null); 
  const [author, setAuthor] = useState(null);
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  
  const [hasUnreadComments, setHasUnreadComments] = useState(false);

  const uid = useId().replace(/:/g, '');
  const pendingLikeRef = useRef(false);
  const lastLocalRef = useRef(0);
  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  const longPressRef = useRef({ timer: null, startX: 0, startY: 0 });
  
  // Для трекинга просмотров
  const cardRef = useRef(null);
  const hasTrackedView = useRef(false);

  const isAuthor = !!(currentUser && post.user_id === currentUser.id);
  const hasCaption = !!(post.name && post.name.trim());

  const requireAuth = () => {
    if (onRequireAuth) onRequireAuth();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => { mountedRef.current = false; };
  }, []);

  // Трекинг просмотров (2 секунды в зоне видимости)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (readOnly || !currentUser || hasTrackedView.current) return;
    
    let timeoutId;
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        const entry = entries[0];
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          timeoutId = setTimeout(async () => {
            if (hasTrackedView.current) return;
            hasTrackedView.current = true;
            
            try {
              await supabase.rpc('track_views_batch', { 
                p_post_ids: [post.id], 
                p_user_id: currentUser.id 
              });
              // Оптимистичное обновление интерфейса
              setViews(v => v + 1); 
            } catch (e) {
              console.error('Ошибка трекинга просмотра:', e);
            }
          }, 2000); // 2 секунды
        } else {
          if (timeoutId) clearTimeout(timeoutId);
        }
      },
      { threshold: 0.8 } // Пост виден на 80%
    );
    
    if (cardRef.current) observer.observe(cardRef.current);
    
    return () => {
      if (timeoutId) clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      if (cardRef.current) observer.unobserve(cardRef.current);
    };
  }, [post.id, currentUser, readOnly]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!post.user_id) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    getProfile(supabase, post.user_id).then((data) => {
      if (!cancelled && mountedRef.current && data) setAuthor(data);
    });
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [post.user_id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (readOnly) return;
    const load = async () => {
      if (pendingLikeRef.current) return;
      const { count } = await supabase
        .from('reactions')
        .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
        .eq('repository_id', post.id);
      if (pendingLikeRef.current || !mountedRef.current) return;
      setLikes(count || 0);

      if (currentUser) {
        const { data } = await supabase
          .from('reactions')
          .select('id')
          .eq('repository_id', post.id)
          .eq('user_id', currentUser.id)
          .maybeSingle();
        if (!pendingLikeRef.current && mountedRef.current) setIsLiked(!!data);
      }
    };
    load();
  }, [post.id, currentUser, readOnly]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (readOnly) return;
    const ch = supabase
      .channel(`post-${post.id}-${uid}`)
      .on('postgres_changes',
        { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'reactions', filter: `repository_id=eq.${post.id}` },
        async () => {
          if (pendingLikeRef.current || !mountedRef.current) return;
          if (Date.now() - lastLocalRef.current < 2000) return;
          const { count } = await supabase
            .from('reactions')
            .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
            .eq('repository_id', post.id);
          if (mountedRef.current) setLikes(count || 0);
        })
      .on('postgres_changes',
        { event: 'UPDATE', schema: 'public', table: 'repositories', filter: `id=eq.${post.id}` },
        (p) => { if (mountedRef.current) setViews(p.new.views || 0); })
      .subscribe();
    return () => supabase.removeChannel(ch);
  }, [post.id, currentUser, uid, readOnly]);

  const handleLike = async () => {
    if (readOnly) { requireAuth(); return; }
    if (!currentUser || pendingLikeRef.current) return;
    pendingLikeRef.current = true;
    lastLocalRef.current = Date.now();

    const wasLiked = isLiked;
    const prevLikes = likes;
    setIsLiked(!wasLiked);
    setLikes(wasLiked ? prevLikes - 1 : prevLikes + 1);

    if (!wasLiked) {
      setBurst(true);
      setTimeout(() => setBurst(false), 600);
    }

    try {
      if (wasLiked) {
        await supabase.from('reactions').delete()
          .eq('repository_id', post.id).eq('user_id', currentUser.id);
      } else {
        await supabase.from('reactions').insert({
          repository_id: post.id,
          user_id: currentUser.id,
          emoji: '❤️',
        });
      }
    } catch {
      if (mountedRef.current) {
        setIsLiked(wasLiked);
        setLikes(prevLikes);
      }
    } finally {
      setTimeout(() => { pendingLikeRef.current = false; }, 500);
    }
  };

  const handleOpenComments = () => {
    if (readOnly) { requireAuth(); return; }
    setHasUnreadComments(false);
    onOpen?.(post);
  };

  const handleCardClick = (e) => {
    if (!readOnly) return;
    if (e.target.closest('button, a, input, textarea, [data-no-redirect]')) return;
    requireAuth();
  };

  const handleContextMenu = (e) => {
    if (readOnly) {
      e.preventDefault();
      requireAuth();
      return;
    }
    e.preventDefault();
    setMenuOpen(true);
  };

  const handlePointerDown = (e) => {
    if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
    if (readOnly) return;
    if (e.target.closest('a') || e.target.closest('.post-header')) return; 

    longPressRef.current.startX = e.clientX;
    longPressRef.current.startY = e.clientY;
    longPressRef.current.timer = setTimeout(() => {
      longPressRef.current.timer = null;
      setMenuOpen(true);
    }, 500);
  };

  const handlePointerMove = (e) => {
    if (readOnly) return;
    const s = longPressRef.current;
    if (!s.timer) return;
    if (Math.abs(e.clientX - s.startX) > 10 || Math.abs(e.clientY - s.startY) > 10) {
      clearTimeout(s.timer);
      s.timer = null;
    }
  };

  const handlePointerEnd = () => {
    if (readOnly) return;
    const s = longPressRef.current;
    if (s.timer) {
      clearTimeout(s.timer);
      s.timer = null;
    }
  };

  const handleShare = async () => {
    const postUrl = `${window.location.origin}/post/${post.id}`;
    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({
          title: post.name || 'Пост в «связь»',
          text: post.name || '',
          url: postUrl,
        });
        return;
      } catch (e) {
        if (e.name === 'AbortError') return;
      }
    }
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(postUrl);
    } catch {
      const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.value = postUrl;
      document.body.appendChild(ta);
      ta.select();
      document.execCommand('copy');
      document.body.removeChild(ta);
    }
  };

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(post.name || '');
    } catch {
      const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.value = post.name || '';
      document.body.appendChild(ta);
      ta.select();
      document.execCommand('copy');
      document.body.removeChild(ta);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!window.confirm('Удалить пост? Это действие необратимо.')) return;
    try {
      const marker = '/repopart-files/';
      const paths = (post.files || [])
        .map((f) => {
          const idx = (f.url || '').indexOf(marker);
          return idx >= 0 ? f.url.slice(idx + marker.length) : null;
        })
        .filter(Boolean);

      if (paths.length) {
        await supabase.storage.from('repopart-files').remove(paths);
      }

      const { error } = await supabase.from('repositories').delete().eq('id', post.id);
      if (error) throw error;
    } catch (e) {
      alert('Ошибка удаления: ' + e.message);
    }
  };

  const fallbackName = post.author_email ? post.author_email.split('@')[0] : 'user';
  const displayName = author?.username || fallbackName;

  const renderTextWithLinks = (text) => {
    if (!text) return null;
    const urlRegex = /(https?:\/\/[^\s]+)/g;
    return text.split(urlRegex).map((part, i) => {
      if (part.match(urlRegex)) {
        return (
          <a
            key={i}
            href={part}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            onClick={(e) => {
              if (e.shiftKey || readOnly) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                if (readOnly) requireAuth();
                else setHtmlPreview({ url: part, name: part });
              }
            }}
            onPointerDown={(e) => {
              if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse') {
                e.currentTarget._timer = setTimeout(() => {
                  e.currentTarget._triggered = true;
                  if (readOnly) requireAuth();
                  else setHtmlPreview({ url: part, name: part });
                }, 500);
              }
            }}
            onPointerUp={(e) => {
              clearTimeout(e.currentTarget._timer);
              if (e.currentTarget._triggered) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                e.currentTarget._triggered = false;
              }
            }}
            onPointerLeave={(e) => clearTimeout(e.currentTarget._timer)}
            onPointerMove={(e) => {
              if (e.currentTarget._timer) clearTimeout(e.currentTarget._timer);
            }}
            style={{ color: 'var(--accent)', textDecoration: 'none', wordBreak: 'break-all' }}
          >
            {part}
          </a>
        );
      }
      return part;
    });
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div
        ref={cardRef}
        className={`post-card ${readOnly ? 'is-preview' : ''}`}
        onClick={handleCardClick}
        onContextMenu={handleContextMenu}
        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}
        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}
        onPointerUp={handlePointerEnd}
        onPointerCancel={handlePointerEnd}
      >
        <PostHeader
          author={author}
          userId={post.user_id}
          displayName={displayName}
          createdAt={post.created_at}
          authorEmail={post.author_email}
          onOpenProfile={onOpenProfile}
        />

        {post.files?.length > 0 && (
          <PostMedia
            files={post.files}
            postName={post.name}
            onPreview={readOnly ? requireAuth : (files, index) => setPreview({ files, index })}
            onOpenHtml={readOnly ? requireAuth : (file) => setHtmlPreview(file)}
          />
        )}

        {hasCaption && <div className="post-text">{renderTextWithLinks(post.name)}</div>}

        <PostFooter
          views={views}
          likes={likes}
          isLiked={isLiked}
          burst={burst}
          onLike={handleLike}
          files={post.files}
          postId={post.id}
          onOpenComments={handleOpenComments}
          hasUnreadComments={hasUnreadComments}
        />
      </div>

      {!readOnly && (
        <PostContextMenu
          open={menuOpen}
          onClose={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
          onShare={handleShare}
          onCopy={handleCopy}
          onDelete={handleDelete}
          isAuthor={isAuthor}
          hasCaption={hasCaption}
        />
      )}

      {preview && !readOnly && createPortal(
        <Lightbox 
          files={preview.files} 
          initialIndex={preview.index} 
          onClose={() => setPreview(null)} 
        />,
        document.body
      )}

      {htmlPreview && !readOnly && createPortal(
        <HtmlPreviewScreen 
          file={htmlPreview} 
          onClose={() => setHtmlPreview(null)} 
        />,
        document.body
      )}
    </>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { ShareIcon, CopyIcon, TrashIcon } from '../../../../assets/icons/Icons';
import './PostContextMenu.css';

export default function PostContextMenu({
  open,
  onClose,
  onShare,
  onCopy,
  onDelete,
  isAuthor,
  hasCaption,
}) {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  const sheetRef = useRef(null);
  const dragRef = useRef({
    startY: 0,
    currentY: 0,
    active: false,
    pointerId: null,
  });
  const [dragOffset, setDragOffset] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => setVisible(true)));
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    } else {
      setVisible(false);
      setDragOffset(0);
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') handleClose(); };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [open]);

  const handleClose = () => {
    setVisible(false);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setDragOffset(0);
      onClose();
    }, 280);
  };

  const handlePointerDown = (e) => {
    if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;
    const target = e.target.closest('.post-ctx-item');
    if (target) return;

    dragRef.current.active = true;
    dragRef.current.startY = e.clientY;
    dragRef.current.currentY = e.clientY;
    dragRef.current.pointerId = e.pointerId;

    try { sheetRef.current?.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch {}
  };

  const handlePointerMove = (e) => {
    if (!dragRef.current.active) return;
    if (e.pointerId !== dragRef.current.pointerId) return;
    const dy = e.clientY - dragRef.current.startY;
    if (dy < 0) return;
    dragRef.current.currentY = e.clientY;
    setDragOffset(dy);
  };

  const handlePointerUp = (e) => {
    if (!dragRef.current.active) return;
    if (e.pointerId !== dragRef.current.pointerId) return;

    try { sheetRef.current?.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch {}

    dragRef.current.active = false;
    const dy = dragRef.current.currentY - dragRef.current.startY;

    if (dy > 120) {
      handleClose();
    } else {
      setDragOffset(0);
    }
  };

  if (!open) return null;

  const translateY = visible ? dragOffset : 400;

  return createPortal(
    <div
      className={`post-ctx-backdrop ${visible ? 'is-visible' : ''}`}
      onClick={handleClose}
    >
      <div
        ref={sheetRef}
        className={`post-ctx-sheet ${visible ? 'is-visible' : ''} ${dragRef.current.active ? 'is-dragging' : ''}`}
        style={{
          transform: `translate(-50%, ${translateY}px)`,
        }}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}
        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}
        onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}
        onPointerCancel={handlePointerUp}
      >
        <div className="post-ctx-handle" />

        <div className="post-ctx-list">
          <button
            className="post-ctx-item"
            onClick={() => { handleClose(); onShare?.(); }}
          >
            <span className="post-ctx-icon"><ShareIcon size={22} /></span>
            Поделиться
          </button>

          {hasCaption && (
            <button
              className="post-ctx-item"
              onClick={() => { handleClose(); onCopy?.(); }}
            >
              <span className="post-ctx-icon"><CopyIcon size={22} /></span>
              Скопировать
            </button>
          )}

          {isAuthor && (
            <button
              className="post-ctx-item is-danger"
              onClick={() => { handleClose(); onDelete?.(); }}
            >
              <span className="post-ctx-icon"><TrashIcon size={22} /></span>
              Удалить
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}

File: src/windows/Feed/components/post/PostFooter.jsx
================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '../../../../lib/supabaseClient';
import { getProfile } from '../../../../lib/profilesCache';
import { HeartFilledIcon, EyeIcon, CommentIcon, ArrowRightIcon, DownloadIcon } from '../../../../assets/icons/Icons';
import Avatar from './Avatar';
import './PostFooter.css';

const formatNumber = (n) => {
  if (n >= 1e6) return (n / 1e6).toFixed(1) + 'M';
  if (n >= 1e3) return (n / 1e3).toFixed(1) + 'K';
  return n;
};

const plural = (n, forms) => {
  const d = n % 10, dd = n % 100;
  if (dd >= 11 && dd <= 14) return forms[2];
  if (d === 1) return forms[0];
  if (d >= 2 && d <= 4) return forms[1];
  return forms[2];
};

export default function PostFooter({ 
  views, 
  likes, 
  isLiked, 
  burst, 
  onLike, 
  postId, 
  files = [],
  onOpenComments,
  hasUnreadComments 
}) {
  const [commentCount, setCommentCount] = useState(0);
  const [recentCommenters, setRecentCommenters] = useState([]);
  const instanceIdRef = useRef(Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!postId) return;
    supabase
      .from('comments')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .eq('repository_id', postId)
      .then(({ count }) => setCommentCount(count || 0));
  }, [postId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!postId) return;
    supabase
      .from('comments')
      .select('user_id')
      .eq('repository_id', postId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(3)
      .then(async ({ data }) => {
        if (!data) return;
        const uniqueIds = [...new Set(data.map(c => c.user_id))].slice(0, 3);
        const profiles = await Promise.all(uniqueIds.map(id => getProfile(supabase, id)));
        setRecentCommenters(profiles.filter(Boolean));
      });
  }, [postId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!postId) return;
    const uid = instanceIdRef.current;
    const ch = supabase
      .channel(`comment-count-${postId}-${uid}`)
      .on('postgres_changes',
        { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'comments', filter: `repository_id=eq.${postId}` },
        async () => {
          const { count } = await supabase
            .from('comments')
            .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
            .eq('repository_id', postId);
          setCommentCount(count || 0);
          
          const { data } = await supabase
            .from('comments')
            .select('user_id')
            .eq('repository_id', postId)
            .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
            .limit(3);
          if (data) {
            const uniqueIds = [...new Set(data.map(c => c.user_id))].slice(0, 3);
            const profiles = await Promise.all(uniqueIds.map(id => getProfile(supabase, id)));
            setRecentCommenters(profiles.filter(Boolean));
          }
        })
      .subscribe();
    return () => supabase.removeChannel(ch);
  }, [postId]);

  const handleComments = (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    onOpenComments?.();
  };

  const handleDownload = async (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const f = files[0];
    if (!f) return;
    const filename = f.name || 'download';
    try {
      const res = await fetch(f.url);
      const blob = await res.blob();
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      const objUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      a.href = objUrl;
      a.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      document.body.removeChild(a);
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(objUrl), 1000);
    } catch {
      window.open(f.url, '_blank');
    }
  };

  const commentText = commentCount === 0 
    ? 'Прокомментировать' 
    : `${commentCount} ${plural(commentCount, ['комментарий', 'комментария', 'комментариев'])}`;

  return (
    <div className="post-footer">
      
      <div className="post-stats-row">
        <button
          className={`post-action like-btn ${isLiked ? 'is-liked' : ''} ${burst ? 'is-bursting' : ''}`}
          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onLike?.(); }}
          aria-label="Нравится"
        >
          <div className="like-icon-wrapper">
            <HeartFilledIcon size={20} className="like-icon" />
          </div>
          <span>{formatNumber(likes)}</span>
        </button>

        <div className="post-actions-right">
          <div className="post-action post-views">
            <EyeIcon size={20} />
            <span>{formatNumber(views)}</span>
          </div>

          {files.length > 0 && (
            <button className="post-action post-download-btn" onClick={handleDownload} aria-label="Скачать">
              <DownloadIcon size={20} />
              <span>Скачать</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <button className="post-comments-btn" onClick={handleComments}>
        <div className="post-comments-left">
          {recentCommenters.length > 0 && (
            <div className="post-commenters-avatars">
              {recentCommenters.map((p, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="post-commenter-avatar">
                  <Avatar url={p.avatar_url} name={p.username} seed={p.id} size={20} />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          <CommentIcon size={20} />
          <span className="post-comments-text">{commentText}</span>
          {hasUnreadComments && <span className="unread-dot" />}
        </div>
        <div className="post-comments-right">
          <ArrowRightIcon size={16} />
        </div>
      </button>

    </div>
  );
}

================
File: src/windows/Feed/components/post/PostHeader.jsx
================
import React from 'react';
import { formatTime } from '../../../../lib/formatTime';
import { VerifiedIcon } from '../../../../assets/icons/Icons';
import Avatar from './Avatar';
import './PostHeader.css';

const VERIFIED_EMAILS = ['obrekos6@gmail.com'];

export default function PostHeader({ author, userId, displayName, createdAt, authorEmail, onOpenProfile }) {
  const isVerified = authorEmail && VERIFIED_EMAILS.includes(authorEmail);

  const handleClick = (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (onOpenProfile) onOpenProfile(userId);
  };

  return (
    <div className="post-header" onClick={handleClick} style={{ cursor: onOpenProfile ? 'pointer' : 'default' }}>
      <Avatar
        url={author?.avatar_url}
        name={displayName}
        seed={userId}
        size={40}
      />
      <div className="post-header-info">
        <div className="post-author-row">
          <span className="post-author">{displayName}</span>
          {isVerified && (
            <VerifiedIcon size={16} className="post-verified" />
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="post-time">{formatTime(createdAt)}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

File: src/windows/Feed/components/post/PostMedia.jsx
================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
import AudioPlayer from './AudioPlayer';
import { MuteIcon, PlayIcon, FileIcon } from '../../../../assets/icons/Icons';
import { computeLayout, MAX_TILES } from '../../../../lib/mediaLayout';
import './PostMedia.css';

const NO_FILES = [];
const FALLBACK_RATIO = 1;
const GIVE_UP_MS = 3500;

const isVisual = (f) => f.type?.startsWith('image/') || f.type?.startsWith('video/');
const isHtml = (f) => f.type === 'text/html' || f.name?.toLowerCase().endsWith('.html');
const isAudio = (f) => f.type?.startsWith('audio/');

const formatDuration = (s) => {
  if (!s || !isFinite(s)) return null;
  const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
  const sec = Math.floor(s % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0');
  return `${m}:${sec}`;
};

const imageUrl = (url, tileW) => {
  if (!url) return url;
  if (url.match(/\.(svg|gif)(\?|$)/i)) return url;
  const px = tileW >= 0.9 ? 1200 : tileW >= 0.45 ? 800 : 500;
  const sep = url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?';
  return `${url}${sep}width=${px}&quality=80&resize=contain&format=webp`;
};

const videoUrl = (url) => (url && !url.includes('#') ? `${url}#t=0.001` : url);

const prefersReducedMotion = () =>
  typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
  window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

const ratioCache = new Map();

function useRatios(files) {
  const [measured, setMeasured] = useState({});

  const report = useCallback((url, ratio, persist = true) => {
    if (persist) ratioCache.set(url, ratio);
    setMeasured((prev) => (prev[url] === ratio ? prev : { ...prev, [url]: ratio }));
  }, []);

  const ratios = files.map((f) => {
    if (f.width > 0 && f.height > 0) return f.width / f.height;
    return ratioCache.get(f.url) ?? measured[f.url] ?? null;
  });

  return { ratios, report };
}

function useAutoplayInView(ref, enabled) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el || !enabled || prefersReducedMotion()) return undefined;

    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) el.play().catch(() => {});
        else el.pause();
      },
      { threshold: 0.4 }
    );
    io.observe(el);
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, [ref, enabled]);
}

function MediaTile({ file, rect, style, alt, extra, onOpen, onRatio }) {
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(null);
  const videoRef = useRef(null);

  const video = file.type.startsWith('video/');
  const hasDims = file.width > 0 && file.height > 0;
  const compact = rect.w < 0.45;

  useAutoplayInView(videoRef, video);

  const handleKey = (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
      e.preventDefault();
      onOpen();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={`post-media-tile ${compact ? 'is-compact' : ''}`} style={style}>
      <div
        className="post-media-tile-inner"
        role="button"
        tabIndex={0}
        onClick={onOpen}
        onKeyDown={handleKey}
      >
        {video ? (
          <video
            ref={videoRef}
            src={videoUrl(file.url)}
            muted
            loop
            playsInline
            preload="metadata"
            disablePictureInPicture
            className={`post-image ${loaded ? 'is-loaded' : ''}`}
            onLoadedMetadata={(e) => {
              const v = e.currentTarget;
              setDuration(v.duration);
              if (!hasDims) {
                onRatio(file.url, v.videoWidth && v.videoHeight ? v.videoWidth / v.videoHeight : FALLBACK_RATIO);
              }
              setLoaded(true);
            }}
            onError={() => {
              if (!hasDims) onRatio(file.url, FALLBACK_RATIO, false);
            }}
          />
        ) : (
          <img
            src={imageUrl(file.url, rect.w)}
            alt={alt}
            loading="lazy"
            decoding="async"
            draggable={false}
            className={`post-image ${loaded ? 'is-loaded' : ''}`}
            onLoad={(e) => {
              const img = e.currentTarget;
              if (!hasDims) {
                onRatio(file.url, img.naturalWidth && img.naturalHeight ? img.naturalWidth / img.naturalHeight : FALLBACK_RATIO);
              }
              setLoaded(true);
            }}
            onError={() => {
              if (!hasDims) onRatio(file.url, FALLBACK_RATIO, false);
            }}
          />
        )}

        {video && loaded && (
          <>
            <div className="post-mute">
              <MuteIcon size={14} />
            </div>
            {formatDuration(duration) && (
              <div className="post-duration">
                <PlayIcon size={12} />
                {formatDuration(duration)}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}

        {extra > 0 && <div className="post-media-more">+{extra}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function PostMedia({ files = NO_FILES, postName, onPreview, onOpenHtml }) {
  const visual = useMemo(() => files.filter(isVisual), [files]);
  const others = useMemo(() => files.filter((f) => !isVisual(f)), [files]);
  const shown = useMemo(() => visual.slice(0, MAX_TILES), [visual]);
  const hiddenCount = visual.length - shown.length;

  const { ratios, report } = useRatios(shown);
  const allKnown = ratios.every((r) => r != null);

  const [gaveUp, setGaveUp] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (allKnown) return undefined;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setGaveUp(true), GIVE_UP_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [allKnown]);
  const ready = allKnown || gaveUp;

  const ratioKey = ratios.map((r) => (r == null ? '_' : r.toFixed(4))).join('|');
  const layout = useMemo(
    () => computeLayout(ratios.map((r) => r ?? FALLBACK_RATIO)),
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [ratioKey]
  );

  if (files.length === 0) return null;

  const H = layout.height || 1;

  return (
    <div className="post-media">
      {shown.length > 0 && (
        <div
          className={`post-media-grid ${ready ? 'is-ready' : 'is-pending'}`}
          style={{ paddingBottom: `${H * 100}%` }}
        >
          <div className="post-media-grid-inner">
            {shown.map((f, i) => {
              const t = layout.tiles[i];
              if (!t) return null;
              return (
                <MediaTile
                  key={f.id || f.url || i}
                  file={f}
                  rect={t}
                  alt={shown.length === 1 ? postName || '' : ''}
                  extra={i === shown.length - 1 ? hiddenCount : 0}
                  style={{
                    left: `${t.x * 100}%`,
                    top: `${(t.y / H) * 100}%`,
                    width: `${t.w * 100}%`,
                    height: `${(t.h / H) * 100}%`,
                  }}
                  onRatio={report}
                  onOpen={() => onPreview?.(f, i, visual)}
                />
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {others.map((f, i) => {
        if (isAudio(f)) {
          return (
            <div className="post-audio" key={f.id || f.url || `a${i}`}>
              <AudioPlayer src={f.url} name={f.name} />
            </div>
          );
        }

        const isHtmlFile = isHtml(f);

        return (
          <div
            className="post-file"
            key={f.id || f.url || `f${i}`}
            role="button"
            tabIndex={0}
            onClick={() => {
              if (isHtmlFile && onOpenHtml) onOpenHtml(f);
              else onPreview?.(f, 0, [f]);
            }}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (isHtmlFile && onOpenHtml) onOpenHtml(f);
                else onPreview?.(f, 0, [f]);
              }
            }}
          >
            <div className="post-file-icon">
              <FileIcon size={40} />
            </div>
            <div className="post-file-name">{f.name}</div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

File: src/windows/Feed/components/post/PublicationList.jsx

import React from 'react';
import PostCard from './PostCard';

export default function PublicationList({
  publications,
  currentUser,
  onOpenPost,
  readOnly = false,
  onRequireAuth,
  onOpenProfile,
}) {
  if (publications.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="empty-state">
        <div className="empty-state-icon">🛰️</div>
        <p className="empty-state-text">Тут тихо...</p>
        <p className="empty-state-subtext">Загляните позже</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="publication-list">
      {publications.map((post) => (
        <PostCard
          key={post.id}
          post={post}
          currentUser={currentUser}
          onOpen={onOpenPost}
          readOnly={readOnly}
          onRequireAuth={onRequireAuth}
          onOpenProfile={onOpenProfile}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

    color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  }
}

.tab.is-active {
  color: #1a1a1a;
  font-weight: var(--font-bold);
}

  .tab.is-active {

================
File: src/windows/Feed/FeedScreen.jsx
================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient';
import Logo from '../../assets/icons/Logo';
import { LogoutIcon, PlusIcon } from '../../assets/icons/Icons';
import SideMenu from './components/layout/SideMenu';
import Tabs from './components/layout/Tabs';
import CreateRepoModal from './components/modals/CreateRepoModal';
import PublicationList from './components/post/PublicationList';
import PostDetailScreen from '../Post/PostDetailScreen';
import ProfileScreen from '../Profile/ProfileScreen';
import './FeedScreen.css';

const POSTS_PER_PAGE = 10;

const NAV_ITEMS = [
  { label: 'Лента', icon: '📡', action: 'feed' },
  { label: 'Профиль', icon: '👤', action: 'profile' },
  { label: 'Мои посты', icon: '📁', action: 'my' },
  { label: 'Настройки', icon: '⚙️', action: 'settings' },
];

const TABS = [
  { id: 'for-you', label: 'Для вас' },
  { id: 'fresh', label: 'Свежее' },
  { id: 'top', label: 'Топ' },
];

export default function FeedScreen() {
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [hasMore, setHasMore] = useState(true);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('for-you');
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState(null);
  const [openPost, setOpenPost] = useState(null);
  
  const [profileOpen, setProfileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [profileUserId, setProfileUserId] = useState(null); // Для чужих профилей
  const [needsProfileSetup, setNeedsProfileSetup] = useState(false);

  const sentinelRef = useRef(null);
  const loadingRef = useRef(false);
  const requestIdRef = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data: { user } }) => setCurrentUser(user));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .select('username')
      .eq('id', currentUser.id)
      .maybeSingle()
      .then(({ data }) => {
        if (!data?.username) setNeedsProfileSetup(true);
      });
  }, [currentUser]);

  const loadPosts = useCallback(async (mode, limit, offset) => {
    if (mode === 'for-you') {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('get_recommended_feed', {
        p_user_id: currentUser?.id || null,
        p_limit: limit,
        p_offset: offset,
      });
      if (error) return [];
      return data || [];
    }

    if (mode === 'top') {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('repositories')
        .select('*')
        .order('hot_score', { ascending: false })
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
        .range(offset, offset + limit - 1);
      return data || [];
    }

    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('repositories')
      .select('*')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .range(offset, offset + limit - 1);
    return data || [];
  }, [currentUser]);

  const load = useCallback(async (reset = false) => {
    if (!reset && !hasMore) return;

    const reqId = ++requestIdRef.current;

    if (reset) {
      loadingRef.current = true;
      setPosts([]);
      setHasMore(true);
      setLoading(true);
    } else {
      if (loadingRef.current) return;
      loadingRef.current = true;
      setLoading(true);
    }

    const offset = reset ? 0 : posts.length;
    const data = await loadPosts(tab, POSTS_PER_PAGE, offset);

    if (reqId !== requestIdRef.current) return;

    if (reset) {
      setPosts(data);
    } else {
      setPosts((prev) => [...prev, ...data]);
    }

    setHasMore(data.length === POSTS_PER_PAGE);
    setLoading(false);
    loadingRef.current = false;
  }, [tab, posts.length, hasMore, loadPosts]);

  useEffect(() => {
    load(true);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [tab, currentUser]);

  const handleLogoClick = useCallback(async () => {
    if (refreshing) return;
    const isAtTop = window.scrollY < 5;

    if (!isAtTop) {
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      return;
    }

    setRefreshing(true);
    const reqId = ++requestIdRef.current;

    try {
      const data = await loadPosts(tab, POSTS_PER_PAGE, 0);
      if (reqId !== requestIdRef.current) return;
      setPosts(data);
      setHasMore(data.length === POSTS_PER_PAGE);
    } catch {}

    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));
    setRefreshing(false);
  }, [refreshing, tab, loadPosts]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sentinelRef.current) return;
    if (!hasMore) return;

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (entries[0].isIntersecting) load(false);
      },
      { rootMargin: '400px' }
    );
    observer.observe(sentinelRef.current);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [hasMore, load, tab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const channel = supabase
      .channel('repositories-feed')
      .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'repositories' },
        (p) => setPosts((prev) => [p.new, ...prev]))
      .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'DELETE', schema: 'public', table: 'repositories' },
        (p) => setPosts((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== p.old.id)))
      .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'UPDATE', schema: 'public', table: 'repositories' },
        (p) => setPosts((prev) => prev.map((x) => x.id === p.new.id ? p.new : x)))
      .subscribe();
    return () => supabase.removeChannel(channel);
  }, []);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
  };

  const handleNavClick = (action) => {
    if (action === 'profile') {
      setProfileUserId(null);
      setProfileOpen(true);
    }
    setMenuOpen(false);
  };

  const handleOpenPost = (post) => {
    setOpenPost(post);
  };

  const handleOpenProfile = (userId) => {
    if (userId === currentUser?.id) {
      setProfileUserId(null);
      setProfileOpen(true);
    } else {
      setProfileUserId(userId);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="app">
        <div className="app-bg" />

        <aside className="desktop-sidebar">
          <ul className="side-menu-list">
            {NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.label}>
                <button
                  className="side-menu-item"
                  onClick={() => handleNavClick(item.action)}
                >
                  <span className="side-menu-icon">{item.icon}</span>
                  {item.label}
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </aside>

        <button className="menu-btn" aria-label="Меню" onClick={() => setMenuOpen(true)}>
          <div className="menu-btn-stripes">
            <span></span><span></span><span></span>
          </div>
        </button>

        <button className="fixed-logo" onClick={handleLogoClick} aria-label="Обновить ленту">
          <Logo className="logo-icon" />
          <span className="logo-text">связь</span>
          <span className="logo-beta-capsule">BETA</span>
        </button>

        <div className="double-btn">
          <button className="double-btn-item" aria-label="Выйти" onClick={handleLogout} title="Выйти">
            <LogoutIcon size={20} />
          </button>
          <button className="double-btn-item" aria-label="Создать" onClick={() => setModalOpen(true)}>
            <PlusIcon size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <main className="main-content">
          <section className="interests-section">
            <Tabs tabs={TABS} active={tab} onChange={setTab} />

            {refreshing && (
              <div className="refresh-overlay">
                <div className="refresh-text">Обновляем рекомендации...</div>
              </div>
            )}

            {loading && posts.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="loading-feed">Загрузка...</div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <PublicationList
                  publications={posts}
                  currentUser={currentUser}
                  onOpenPost={handleOpenPost}
                  onOpenProfile={handleOpenProfile}
                />
                {hasMore && <div ref={sentinelRef} className="load-more-sentinel" />}
              </>
            )}
          </section>
        </main>
      </div>

      <SideMenu
        open={menuOpen}
        onClose={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
        items={NAV_ITEMS}
        onItemClick={handleNavClick}
      />

      {modalOpen && (
        <CreateRepoModal onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)} onCreate={() => load(true)} />
      )}

      {openPost && currentUser && (
        <PostDetailScreen
          post={openPost}
          currentUser={currentUser}
          onClose={() => setOpenPost(null)}
        />
      )}

      {needsProfileSetup && currentUser ? (
        <ProfileScreen
          user={currentUser}
          initialEdit={true}
          forceSetup={true}
          onClose={() => {}}
          onComplete={() => setNeedsProfileSetup(false)}
        />
      ) : profileOpen && currentUser ? (
        <ProfileScreen
          user={currentUser}
          onClose={() => setProfileOpen(false)}
          onOpenPost={handleOpenPost}
        />
      ) : profileUserId ? (
        <ProfileScreen
          user={currentUser}
          targetUserId={profileUserId}
          onClose={() => setProfileUserId(null)}
          onOpenPost={handleOpenPost}
        />
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient';
import Logo from '../../assets/icons/Logo';
import { UserIcon, PlusIcon } from '../../assets/icons/Icons';
import PublicationList from '../Feed/components/post/PublicationList';
import SideMenu from '../Feed/components/layout/SideMenu';
import Tabs from '../Feed/components/layout/Tabs';
import './LandingScreen.css';

const POSTS_PER_PAGE = 10;

const NAV_ITEMS = [
  { label: 'Лента', icon: '📡', action: 'feed' },
    { label: 'Профиль', icon: '👤', action: 'profile' },
      { label: 'Мои посты', icon: '📁', action: 'my' },
        { label: 'Настройки', icon: '⚙️', action: 'settings' },
        ];

        const TABS = [
          { id: 'for-you', label: 'Для вас' },
            { id: 'fresh', label: 'Свежее' },
              { id: 'top', label: 'Топ' },
              ];

              export default function LandingScreen({ user, loading }) {
                const navigate = useNavigate();
                  const [posts, setPosts] = useState([]);
                    const [postsLoading, setPostsLoading] = useState(true);
                      const [hasMore, setHasMore] = useState(true);
                        const [tab, setTab] = useState('for-you');
                          const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);

                            const sentinelRef = useRef(null);
                              const loadingRef = useRef(false);

                                const authed = !loading && user;

                                  /* auto-redirect logged-in users */
                                    useEffect(() => {
                                        if (!loading && user) {
                                              navigate('/feed', { replace: true });
                                                  }
                                                    }, [loading, user, navigate]);

                                                      const goAuth = useCallback(() => navigate('/auth'), [navigate]);
                                                        const goFeed = useCallback(() => navigate('/feed'), [navigate]);
                                                          const guestClick = useCallback(
                                                              () => (authed ? goFeed() : goAuth()),
                                                                  [authed, goAuth, goFeed]
                                                                    );

                                                                      /* posts loader */
                                                                        const loadPosts = useCallback(async (mode, limit, offset) => {
                                                                            if (mode === 'top') {
                                                                                  const { data } = await supabase
                                                                                          .from('repositories')
                                                                                                  .select('*')
                                                                                                          .order('hot_score', { ascending: false, nullsFirst: false })
                                                                                                                  .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
                                                                                                                          .range(offset, offset + limit - 1);
                                                                                                                                return data || [];
                                                                                                                                    }

                                                                                                                                        const { data } = await supabase
                                                                                                                                              .from('repositories')
                                                                                                                                                    .select('*')
                                                                                                                                                          .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
                                                                                                                                                                .range(offset, offset + limit - 1);
                                                                                                                                                                    return data || [];
                                                                                                                                                                      }, []);

                                                                                                                                                                        const load = useCallback(async (reset = false) => {
                                                                                                                                                                            if (!reset && !hasMore) return;
                                                                                                                                                                                if (loadingRef.current) return;

                                                                                                                                                                                    loadingRef.current = true;
                                                                                                                                                                                        if (reset) {
                                                                                                                                                                                              setPosts([]);
                                                                                                                                                                                                    setPostsLoading(true);
                                                                                                                                                                                                          setHasMore(true);
                                                                                                                                                                                                              }

                                                                                                                                                                                                                  const offset = reset ? 0 : posts.length;
                                                                                                                                                                                                                      const data = await loadPosts(tab, POSTS_PER_PAGE, offset);

                                                                                                                                                                                                                          if (reset) setPosts(data);
                                                                                                                                                                                                                              else setPosts((prev) => [...prev, ...data]);

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  setHasMore(data.length === POSTS_PER_PAGE);
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      setPostsLoading(false);
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          loadingRef.current = false;
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            }, [tab, posts.length, hasMore, loadPosts]);

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              useEffect(() => {
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  load(true);
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    }, [tab, load]);

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      /* infinite scroll */
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        useEffect(() => {
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            if (!sentinelRef.current || !hasMore) return;
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      (entries) => {
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              if (entries[0].isIntersecting) load(false);

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              if (window.scrollY > 5) {
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          return;
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              }
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  load(true);
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    }, [load]);

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          return;
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              }
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  guestClick();
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    }, [guestClick]);

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                <div className="app">
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        <div className="app-bg" />

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      className="side-menu-item"
